We can then proceed with Lemma
6.18
:

\displaystyle\sup_{s}\int_{0}^{s}\left|\left(2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\left(X_{F}(1,t;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

(6.11)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}|\psi(1,t)|+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{1}(t,t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t;\tau)^{-3}v_{B}\left(X_{F}(1,t;\tau)\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}|\psi(1,t)|+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{2\underline{c}}{e^{C\kappa}}\int_{\xi_{0}(t)}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t;\sigma_{1}(\eta,t))^{-3}v_{B}\left(X_{B}(\eta,0;\sigma_{1}(\eta,t))\right)d\eta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}|\psi(1,t)|+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{5}{2}}}e^{C\kappa}\int_{\xi_{0}(t)}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}e^{-C\kappa}(\eta-\xi_{0}(t))\right)^{-3}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

6.2
Estimate of (
6.7
)

By continuity, for each
\eta\in[\xi,\xi_{0}(T)]
, there exists a unique
\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)\in[0,T-t]
such that (figure
3
)

X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi))=X_{B}(\eta,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)),\quad\sigma_{2}(\xi,\xi)=0.

In particular, for each
t_{*}\leq T
such that
\xi_{0}(t_{*})\geq\xi
,
s_{2}(\xi,t_{*}):=\sigma_{2}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),\xi)
satisfies

X_{F}(\xi,0;s_{2}(\xi,t_{*}))=X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})),

so the integrals in (
6.7
) are taken over
\sigma\in(0,s_{2}(\xi,t_{*}))
.

Lemma 6.19
.

For each
\xi\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]
,
\sigma_{2}(\cdot,\xi)
is a strictly increasing
C^{1}
function on
(\xi,\xi_{0}(T))
. Moreover, for
\eta\in(\xi,\xi_{0}(T))
, it holds

\left(2\overline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{-C\kappa}\leq\frac{d\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)}{d\eta}\leq\left(2\underline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{C\kappa},

\xi+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\eta-\xi)\leq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi))\leq\xi+\frac{\overline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\eta-\xi).

Proof.

The implicit function theorem and the
H^{2}
regularity of
c
give the
C^{1}
regularity of
\sigma_{2}(\cdot,\xi)
. Assume otherwise
\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi)\leq\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi)
for some
\eta_{2}>\eta_{1}
. Then

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi))=X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi))\leq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi))\leq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi)),

while
X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;0)>X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;0)
, which is contradictory with the uniqueness of integral curves. Hence
\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},t)>\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},t)
and for the same reason
X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\tau)>X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\tau)
for
\tau
in the common interval of
X_{B}(\eta_{1},t;\cdot)
and
X_{B}(\eta_{2},t;\cdot)
. By the same argument as in Lemma
6.18
, for
\eta_{2},\eta_{1}\in[\xi,\xi_{0}(T)]
with
\eta_{2}>\eta_{1}
it holds

e^{-C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1})\leq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},t))-X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},t))\leq e^{C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}).

Use the rough bound (
3.10
) (figure
3
):

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi))+\underline{c}\left(\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi)-\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi)\right)\leq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi))\leq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi))-\underline{c}\left(\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi)-\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi)\right),

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi))+\overline{c}\left(\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi)-\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi)\right)\geq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi))\geq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi))-\overline{c}\left(\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi)-\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi)\right).

Combining the above inequalities yields

\left(2\overline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{-C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1})\leq\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi)-\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi)\leq\left(2\underline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}).

Figure 3:
Positions of the points
X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi))
,
X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{2},\xi))
and
X_{B}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{2}(\eta_{1},\xi))

Since
\eta_{2}
,
\eta_{1}
can be chosen arbitrarily, we obtain

\left(2\overline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{-C\kappa}\leq\frac{d\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)}{d\eta}\leq\left(2\underline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{C\kappa}.

The estimate of
X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi))
follows from the following inequalities:

\displaystyle\max\left\{\xi+\underline{c}\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi),\eta-\overline{c}\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi))=X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\min\left\{\xi+\overline{c}\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi),\eta-\underline{c}\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)\right\},

\frac{\overline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}\left(\xi+\underline{c}\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)\right)+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}\left(\eta-\overline{c}\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)\right)=\xi+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\eta-\xi),

\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}\left(\xi+\overline{c}\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)\right)+\frac{\overline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}\left(\eta-\underline{c}\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi)\right)=\xi+\frac{\overline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\eta-\xi).

∎

By the bound (
3.7
) and Lemma
6.19
, the second line in (
6.7
) can be controlled by

\displaystyle\sup_{s}\int_{0}^{s}\left|\left(\frac{\partial_{t}c+cr^{2}\xi^{-2}\partial_{\xi}c}{2c}\right)\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma)\right)\right|v_{B}\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma)\right)d\sigma

(6.12)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\int_{0}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma)^{-1}v_{B}\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma)\right)d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Ce^{C\kappa}\epsilon}{\underline{c}}\int_{\xi}^{\xi_{0}(T)}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi))^{-1}v_{B}\left(X_{B}(\eta,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi))\right)d\eta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Ce^{C\kappa}\epsilon}{\underline{c}}\int_{\xi}^{\xi_{0}(T)}\left(\xi+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\eta-\xi)\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

Use the bound (
3.6
) and that
\left(\partial_{t}+cr^{2}\partial_{x}\right)\psi=w_{B}
to estimate the third line in (
6.7
):

\displaystyle\sup_{s}\int_{0}^{s}\left|\left(2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma)^{-4}\left|\psi\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma)^{-4}\left|\psi(\xi,0)+\int_{0}^{\sigma}w_{B}\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\tau)\right)d\tau\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma)^{-4}d\sigma|\psi(\xi,0)|

\displaystyle+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}\int_{0}^{\sigma}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma)^{-4}v_{B}\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\tau)\right)d\tau d\sigma

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma)^{-4}d\sigma|\psi(\xi,0)|

\displaystyle+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}\left(\int_{\tau}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma)^{-4}d\sigma\right)v_{B}\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\tau)\right)d\tau.

Note that the rough bound (
3.10
) yields

\displaystyle\int_{\tau}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\sigma)^{-4}d\sigma\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\tau}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}\left(X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\tau)+\underline{c}(\sigma-\tau)\right)^{-4}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3\underline{c}}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\tau)^{-3}.

Then we proceed by Lemma
6.19
and obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{s}\int_{0}^{s}\left|\left(2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

(6.13)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\xi^{-3}|\psi(\xi,0)|+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{2}(\xi,t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\xi,0;\tau)^{-3}v_{B}\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\tau)\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\xi^{-3}|\psi(\xi,0)|+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{2\underline{c}}{e^{C\kappa}}\int_{\xi}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi))^{-3}v_{B}\left(X_{B}(\eta,0;\sigma_{2}(\eta,\xi))\right)d\eta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\xi^{-3}|\psi(\xi,0)|+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{5}{2}}}e^{C\kappa}\int_{\xi}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(\xi+\underline{c}\left(\overline{c}+\underline{c}\right)^{-1}(\eta-\xi)\right)^{-3}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

6.3
Estimate of (
6.8
)

In fact, we will take
\xi=\xi_{0}(t_{*})
in (
6.8
) for the later analysis, and thus it is convenient to establish the estimate with
\xi
replaced by
\xi_{0}(t_{*})
in (
6.8
) with
t_{*}\in[0,T]
, in which case the integrals in (
6.8
) is in fact taken over
\sigma\in(0,t_{*})
by the construction of
\xi_{0}(\cdot)
. For each
\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(t_{*})]
, by continuity, there exists a unique
\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*})\in[0,t_{*}]
such that (figure
4
)

X_{F}(\eta,0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}))=X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*})),\quad\sigma_{3}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),t_{*})=0.

In particular,
s_{3}(t_{*}):=\sigma_{3}(1,t_{*})
satisfies

X_{F}(1,0;s_{3}(t_{*}))=X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;s_{3}(t_{*})).

Analogously, for each
t\in[0,t_{*}]
there exists a unique
\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})\in[t,t_{*}]
such that (figure
5
)

X_{F}(1,t;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t)=X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})),\quad\sigma_{4}(t_{*},t_{*})=t_{*},\quad\sigma_{4}(0,t_{*})=s_{3}(t_{*}).

Recall that
X_{F}(1,t;\sigma_{1}(\eta,t))=X_{B}(\eta,0;t+\sigma_{1}(\eta,t))
for
t\in[0,t]
and
\eta\in[\xi_{0}(t),\xi_{0}(t_{*})]
. Hence by taking
\eta=\xi_{0}(t_{*})
, we have in particular

\sigma_{1}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),t)=\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t.

Lemma 6.20
.

For each
t_{*}\in[0,T]
,
\sigma_{3}(\cdot,t_{*})
is a strictly decreasing
C^{1}
function on
(1,\xi_{0}(t_{*}))
, and
\sigma_{4}(\cdot,t_{*})
is a strictly decreasing
C^{1}
function on
(0,t_{*})
. Moreover, the following estimates hold:

\left(2\overline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{-C\kappa}\leq-\frac{d\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*})}{d\eta}\leq\left(2\underline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{C\kappa},\quad\eta\in(1,\xi_{0}(t_{*})),

\left(2\overline{c}\right)^{-1}\underline{c}e^{-C\kappa}\leq\frac{d\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})}{dt}\leq(2\underline{c})^{-1}\overline{c}e^{C\kappa},\quad t\in(0,t_{*}),

X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}))\geq\eta+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)\geq 1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1),

X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}))\leq\eta+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta),

1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\leq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))\leq 1+\frac{\overline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t).

Proof.

The
C^{1}
regularity of
\sigma_{3}(\cdot,t_{*})
and
\sigma_{4}(\cdot,t_{*})
follows from the implicit function theorem and the
H^{2}
regularity of
c
. Let
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}\in[1,\xi_{0}(t_{*})]
with
\eta_{2}>\eta_{1}
and
t_{1},t_{2}\in[0,t_{*}]
with
t_{2}>t_{1}
. To show the monotonicity, assume otherwise
\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*})\geq\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*})
. Then

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*}))=X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*}))\geq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))\geq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*})),

while
X_{F}(\eta_{1},0;0)<X_{F}(\eta_{2},0;0)
, which is impossible by the uniqueness of integral curves.
Similarly, if we assume
\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})\geq\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})
, then the inequalities

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{2};\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})-t_{2})=X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*}))\geq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{1};\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{1})\geq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{1};\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})-t_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(X_{F}(1,t_{1};t_{2}-t_{1});\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})-t_{2})),

1=X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{2};0)<X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{1};t_{2}-t_{1})=X_{F}^{(\xi)}(X_{F}(1,t_{1};t_{2}-t_{1});0)

lead to contradiction.
By recalling (
3.6
)(
3.7
) we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\tau)-X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\tau)\right)\right|=

\displaystyle\left|\left(cr^{2}\xi^{-2}\right)\left(X_{F}(\eta_{2},0;\tau)\right)-\left(cr^{2}\xi^{-2}\right)\left(X_{F}(\eta_{1},0;\tau)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\tau)^{-1}\left(X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\tau)-X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\tau)\right).

By integrating the above inequality and noting that (
3.11
) gives

\displaystyle C\epsilon\int_{0}^{\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*})}X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\tau)^{-1}d\tau\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\int_{0}^{\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*})}\left(X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;0)+\underline{c}\tau\right)^{-1}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon}{\underline{c}}\log\left(1+\eta_{1}^{-1}\underline{c}\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\kappa,

we obtain

e^{-C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1})\leq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))-X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*}))\leq e^{C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}).

Figure 4:
Positions of the points
X_{F}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*}))
,
X_{F}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))
and
X_{F}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))

Use the rough bound (
3.10
) (figure
4
):

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))-\overline{c}(\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*})-\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))\leq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*}))\leq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2}))+\overline{c}(\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*})-\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*})),

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))-\underline{c}(\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*})-\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))\geq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*}))\geq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2}))+\underline{c}(\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*})-\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*})).

Figure 5:
Positions of the points
X_{F}(1,t_{1};t_{2}-t_{1})
,
X_{F}(1,t_{1};\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{1})
,
X_{F}(1,t_{2};\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{2})
and
X_{F}(1,t_{2};\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})-t_{2})

Combining the above inequalities and recalling that
X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))=X_{F}(\eta_{2},0;\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*}))
, it follows

\left(2\overline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{-C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1})\leq\sigma_{3}(\eta_{1},t_{*})-\sigma_{3}(\eta_{2},t_{*})\leq\left(2\underline{c}\right)^{-1}e^{C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}),

which in turn gives the expected estimate of
\frac{d\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*})}{d\eta}
. To obtain the estimate of
\frac{d\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})}{dt}
,
using the relation

X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{1};\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{1})=X_{F}^{(\xi)}\left(X_{F}(1,t_{1};t_{2}-t_{1});\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{2})\right),

it can be shown in a similar way that

\displaystyle e^{-C\kappa}\left(X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{1};t_{2}-t_{1})-1\right)\leq

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{1};\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{1})-X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{2};\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{C\kappa}\left(X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{1};t_{2}-t_{1})-1\right).

The rough bound (
3.10
) yields (figure
5
)

\underline{c}(t_{2}-t_{1})\leq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{1};t_{2}-t_{1})-1\leq\overline{c}(t_{2}-t_{1}),

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{2};\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{2})+\underline{c}\left(\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})-\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})\right)\leq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{2};\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*}))\leq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*}))-\underline{c}\left(\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})-\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})\right),

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{2};\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{2})+\overline{c}\left(\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})-\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})\right)\geq X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t_{2};\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*}))\geq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*}))-\overline{c}\left(\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})-\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})\right).

Combining the above inequalities and using the relation

X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*}))=X_{F}(1,t_{1};\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})-t_{1}),

we obtain

\left(2\overline{c}\right)^{-1}\underline{c}e^{-C\kappa}(t_{2}-t_{1})\leq\sigma_{4}(t_{2},t_{*})-\sigma_{4}(t_{1},t_{*})\leq\left(2\underline{c}\right)^{-1}\overline{c}e^{C\kappa},

which gives the claimed estimate of
\frac{d\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})}{dt}
.
Next, we have by the rough bound (
3.10
)

\displaystyle\max\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\overline{c}\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}),\eta+\underline{c}\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}))=X_{F}^{(\xi)}(\eta,0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\min\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\underline{c}\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}),\eta+\overline{c}\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*})\right\},

and thus

X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}))\geq\eta+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)\geq 1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1),

X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}))\leq\eta+\frac{\overline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta).

For
X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})
, recall that

X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;t_{*})=(1,t_{*})
, and thus

\displaystyle 1+\underline{c}(t_{*}-\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))\leq X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{B}(1,t_{*};\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t_{*})\leq 1+\overline{c}(t_{*}-\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})).

It follows that

\displaystyle\max\left\{1+\underline{c}(t_{*}-\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})),1+\underline{c}(\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle X_{F}^{(\xi)}(1,t;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))=X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\min\left\{1+\overline{c}(t_{*}-\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}),1+\overline{c}(\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t)\right\}.

Therefore,

1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\leq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))\leq 1+\frac{\overline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t).

∎

Now we estimate the second line in (
6.8
) by using (
3.7
), Lemma
6.20
and splitting the integral into two parts in which
\sigma\in(0,s_{3}(t_{*}))
and
\sigma\in(s_{3}(t_{*}),t_{*})
:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}\left|\frac{\left(\partial_{t}-cr^{2}\xi^{-2}\partial_{\xi}\right)c}{2c}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma\right)\right|v_{F}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma);t_{*}\right)d\sigma

(6.14)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)^{-1}v_{F}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma);t_{*}\right)d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Ce^{C\kappa}\epsilon}{\underline{c}}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}))^{-1}v_{F}\left(X_{F}(\eta,0;\sigma_{3}(\eta,t_{*}));t_{*}\right)d\eta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Ce^{C\kappa}\epsilon}{\underline{c}}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)\right)^{-1}v_{F}(\eta,0;t_{*})d\eta,

\displaystyle\int_{s_{3}(t_{*})}^{t_{*}}\left|\frac{\left(\partial_{t}-cr^{2}\xi^{-2}\partial_{\xi}\right)c}{2c}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma\right)\right|v_{F}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma);t_{*}\right)d\sigma

(6.15)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\int_{s_{3}(t_{*})}^{t_{*}}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)^{-1}v_{F}(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma);t_{*})d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\overline{c}e^{C\kappa}\epsilon}{\underline{c}}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))^{-1}v_{F}\left(X_{F}(\eta,0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}));t_{*}\right)dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\overline{c}e^{C\kappa}\epsilon}{\underline{c}}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}v_{F}(\eta,0;t_{*})dt.

We also split the integral in the third line of (
6.8
) into two parts:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left|2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}\left|2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma+\int_{s_{3}(t_{*})}^{t_{*}}\left|2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma.

We use
(\partial_{t}-cr^{2}\partial_{x})\psi=w_{F}
and (
3.6
)(
3.10
) to estimate the first part:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}\left|2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)^{-4}\left|\psi(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))+\int_{0}^{\sigma}w_{F}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\tau)\right)d\tau\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)^{-4}\left|\psi(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)^{-4}\int_{0}^{\sigma}v_{F}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\tau);t_{*}\right)d\tau d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)^{-4}\left|\psi(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}\left(\int_{\tau}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)^{-4}d\sigma\right)v_{F}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\tau);t_{*}\right)d\tau.

By the rough bound (
3.10
) and Lemma
6.20
, we have

X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\geq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;s_{3}(t_{*}))+\underline{c}(s_{3}(t_{*})-\sigma),

\displaystyle\int_{\tau}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)^{-4}d\sigma\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3\underline{c}}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;s_{3}(t_{*}))^{-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3\underline{c}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)\right)^{-3}.

Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}\left|2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

(6.16)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)\right)^{-3}\left|\psi(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)\right)^{-3}\int_{0}^{s_{3}(t_{*})}v_{F}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\tau);t_{*}\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)\right)^{-3}\left|\psi(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{5}{2}}}e^{C\kappa}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)\right)^{-3}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}v_{F}(\eta,0;t_{*})d\eta.

For the second part, we first use
(\partial_{t}+cr^{2}\partial_{x})\psi=w_{B}
, (
3.6
) and Lemma
6.20
:

\displaystyle\int_{s_{3}(t_{*})}^{t_{*}}\left|2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{s_{3}(t_{*})}^{t_{*}}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)^{-4}\left|\psi\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{C\kappa}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))^{-4}\left|\psi\left(X_{F}(1,t;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t)\right)\right|dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{C\kappa}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))^{-4}\left|\psi(1,t)+\int_{0}^{\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t}w_{B}\left(X_{F}(1,t;\tau)\right)d\tau\right|dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{C\kappa}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-4}\left|\psi(1,t)\right|dt

\displaystyle+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{C\kappa}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))^{-4}\int_{0}^{\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t}v_{B}\left(X_{F}(1,t;\tau)\right)d\tau dt.

We further apply Lemma
6.18
and recall
\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t=\sigma_{1}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),t)
:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t}v_{B}\left(X_{F}(1,t;\tau)\right)d\tau dt\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{2\underline{c}}\int_{\xi_{0}(t)}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}v_{B}\left(X_{B}(\eta,0;t+\sigma_{1}(\eta,t))\right)d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{2\underline{c}}\int_{\xi_{0}(t)}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{*}}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))^{-4}\int_{0}^{\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*})-t}v_{B}\left(X_{F}(1,t;\tau)\right)d\tau dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{2\underline{c}}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))^{-4}\int_{\xi_{0}(t)}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{2\underline{c}}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(\int_{0}^{\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))^{-4}dt\right)v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta,

where
\xi_{0}^{-1}
denotes the inverse function of
\xi_{0}
. Use Lemma
6.20
to estimate the integral in the bracket:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma_{4}(t,t_{*}))^{-4}dt\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-4}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3\underline{c}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left(t_{*}-\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)\right)\right)^{-3}.

Combining the above inequalities yields

\displaystyle\int_{s_{3}(t_{*})}^{t_{*}}\left|2r^{-2}u^{2}\frac{\psi}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma

(6.17)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{C\kappa}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-4}\left|\psi(1,t)\right|dt

\displaystyle+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}\overline{c}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{7}{2}}}e^{C\kappa}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left(t_{*}-\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)\right)\right)^{-3}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

6.4
Collection of (
6.6
-
6.17
) and the estimate of backward pressure wave on the boundary

We now collect all the bounds obtained in this section. First we note in addition that from the bound (
3.6
)(
3.10
) and the smallness
\epsilon\leq\epsilon_{0}\lesssim 1
it follows

\exp\left\{\int_{0}^{s}\left|\frac{2u}{r}\left(X_{F}(1,t;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma\right\}\leq\exp\left\{C\epsilon\int_{0}^{s}\left(1+\underline{c}\sigma\right)^{-2}d\sigma\right\}\leq e^{C\epsilon}\leq 1+C\epsilon,

\exp\left\{\int_{0}^{s}\left|\frac{2u}{r}\left(X_{F}(\xi,0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma\right\}\leq\exp\left\{C\epsilon\int_{0}^{s}\left(\xi+\underline{c}\sigma\right)^{-2}d\sigma\right\}\leq e^{C\epsilon}\leq 1+C\epsilon,

\exp\left\{\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left|\frac{2u}{r}\left(X_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0;\sigma)\right)\right|d\sigma\right\}\leq\exp\left\{C\epsilon\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\underline{c}\sigma\right)^{-2}d\sigma\right\}\leq e^{C\epsilon}\leq 1+C\epsilon.

For simplicity, we write
\mu:=\frac{\overline{c}}{\underline{c}}e^{C\kappa}>1
. Combining (
6.6
)(
6.10
)(
6.11
) gives

\displaystyle v_{F}(1,t;t_{*})

(6.18)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+C\epsilon)\left|\frac{w_{F}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(1,t)\right|+C\epsilon\int_{\xi_{0}(t)}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\eta-\xi_{0}(t))\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta+C\epsilon^{2}|\psi(1,t)|.

Analogously, from (
6.7
)(
6.12
)(
6.13
) it holds

\displaystyle v_{F}(\xi,0;t_{*})

(6.19)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+C\epsilon)\left|\frac{w_{F}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi,0)\right|+C\epsilon\int_{\xi}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(\xi+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\eta-\xi)\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta+C\epsilon^{2}\xi^{-3}|\psi(\xi,0)|,

Since
1+\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)\geq\frac{\underline{c}}{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}\xi_{0}(t_{*})
, from (
6.8
), (
6.14
-
6.17
) it follows

\displaystyle v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)

(6.20)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+C\epsilon)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{2}\xi_{0}(t_{*})^{-3}|\psi(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))|+C\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-4}|\psi(1,t)|dt

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\xi_{0}(t_{*})^{-1}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}v_{F}(\xi,0;t_{*})d\xi+C\epsilon\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}v_{F}(1,t;t_{*})dt

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{2}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left(t_{*}-\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)\right)\right)^{-3}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

We further call upon (
6.5
) to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(1,t_{*}\right)-\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0\right)\right|

(6.21)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon|w_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)|+C\epsilon^{2}\xi_{0}(t_{*})^{-3}|\psi(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))|+C\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-4}|\psi(1,t)|dt

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\xi_{0}(t_{*})^{-1}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}v_{F}(\xi,0;t_{*})d\xi+C\epsilon\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}v_{F}(1,t;t_{*})dt

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{2}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left(t_{*}-\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)\right)\right)^{-3}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

To obtain an explicit control of the last terms in (
6.20
) and (
6.21
), we have the following control of
\xi_{0}^{-1}
:

Figure 6:
Position of the point
X_{B}(\eta_{2},0;\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1}))

Lemma 6.21
.

For each
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]
with
\eta_{1}<\eta_{2}
, the following inequality holds:

\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{\underline{c}}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1})\geq\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{2})-\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1})\geq\frac{e^{-C\kappa}}{\overline{c}}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}).

In particular, it follows that

\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{\underline{c}}\geq\frac{d\xi_{0}^{-1}}{d\eta}\geq\frac{e^{-C\kappa}}{\overline{c}},

t_{*}-\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)\geq\frac{e^{-C\kappa}}{\overline{c}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta).

Proof.

By simulating the proof of Lemma
6.18
we have

e^{C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1})\geq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1}))-X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1}))\geq e^{-C\kappa}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}).

Then use the rough bound (
3.10
) (figure
6
):

\displaystyle\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{2})-\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1})\geq\frac{1}{\overline{c}}\left(X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1}))-X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1})\right)\geq\frac{e^{-C\kappa}}{\overline{c}}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}),

\displaystyle\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{2})-\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1})\leq\frac{1}{\underline{c}}\left(X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{2},0;\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1}))-X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\eta_{1},0;\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta_{1})\right)\leq\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{\underline{c}}(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}).

∎

From the above lemma, we see that

\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left(t_{*}-\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)\right)\right)^{-3}\geq\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)\right)^{-3}.

Then replacing
v_{F}(\eta,0;t_{*})
and
v_{F}(1,t;t_{*})
in (
6.20
) by the bounds (
6.18
)(
6.19
) gives

\displaystyle v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)

(6.22)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+C\epsilon)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{2}\xi_{0}(t_{*})^{-3}|\psi(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))|+C\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-4}|\psi(1,t)|dt

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{3}\xi_{0}(t_{*})^{-1}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\eta^{-3}|\psi(\eta,0)|d\eta+C\epsilon^{3}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}|\psi(1,t)|dt

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\xi_{0}(t_{*})^{-1}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}|w_{F}(\eta,0)|d\eta+C\epsilon\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}|w_{F}(1,t)|dt

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{2}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)\right)^{-3}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{2}\xi_{0}(t_{*})^{-1}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\int_{\xi}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(\xi+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\eta-\xi)\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta d\xi

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\int_{\xi_{0}(t)}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\eta-\xi_{0}(t))\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta dt.

Since
w_{F}=\left(\partial_{t}-cr^{2}\xi^{-2}\partial_{\xi}\right)\psi=2\partial_{t}\psi-w_{B}
, it holds

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}|w_{F}(1,t)|dt

(6.23)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}|\partial_{t}\psi(1,t)|dt+\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t),0)dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}|\partial_{t}\psi(1,t)|dt

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{C\kappa}\overline{c}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{c}}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

The last inequality follows from Lemma
6.21
.
To simplify the last two terms in (
6.22
) we compute

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\int_{\xi}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(\xi+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\eta-\xi)\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta d\xi

(6.24)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(\int_{1}^{\eta}\left(\xi+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\eta-\xi)\right)^{-1}d\xi\right)v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\log\left(\frac{\eta}{1+\frac{1}{2\mu}\left(\eta-1\right)}\right)v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta,

and use Lemma
6.21
to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\int_{\xi_{0}(t)}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\eta-\xi_{0}(t))\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(\int_{0}^{\xi_{0}^{-1}(\eta)}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\eta-\xi_{0}(t))\right)^{-1}dt\right)v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{\underline{c}}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(\int_{1}^{\eta}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}\left(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\xi\right)\right)^{-1}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}\left(\eta-\xi\right)\right)^{-1}d\xi\right)v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{\underline{c}}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\frac{4\mu^{2}}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta}\left(\log\frac{2\mu+\eta-1}{2\mu}-\log\frac{2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1}{2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta}\right)v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{C\kappa}}{\underline{c}}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\frac{4\mu^{2}}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta}\log\left(1+\frac{(\eta-1)(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)}{4\mu^{2}+2\mu(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)}\right)v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

If
\eta\in\left(1,\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1\right)
, using the bound (
3.10
)(
3.11
) the following holds:

\frac{1}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta}\leq\frac{1+2\mu}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta+2\mu},

\log\left(1+\frac{(\eta-1)(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)}{2\mu(2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)}\right)\leq\log\xi_{0}(t_{*})\leq\log(1+\overline{c}t_{*})\leq\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon},

and thus

\frac{4\mu^{2}}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta}\log\left(1+\frac{(\eta-1)(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)}{4\mu^{2}+2\mu(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)}\right)\leq\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\frac{4\mu^{2}(1+2\mu)}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta+2\mu}.

If
\eta\in\left[\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1,\xi_{0}(t_{*})\right)
, we have

\frac{4\mu^{2}}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta}\log\left(1+\frac{(\eta-1)(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)}{4\mu^{2}+2\mu(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)}\right)\leq\frac{2\mu(\eta-1)}{2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1}\leq\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\frac{2\mu(2\mu+1)}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta+2\mu}.

Hence we obtain in each case

\frac{4\mu^{2}}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta}\log\left(1+\frac{(\eta-1)(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta)}{4\mu^{2}+2\mu(\xi_{0}(t_{*})-1)}\right)\lesssim\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\frac{1}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta+2\mu},

and thus

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\int_{\xi_{0}(t)}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}(\eta-\xi_{0}(t)))\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta dt

(6.25)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Ce^{C\kappa}}{\underline{c}}\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\frac{1}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta+2\mu}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

From (
6.23
)(
6.24
)(
6.25
) we see that all the terms on the right-hand side of (
6.22
) involving
v_{B}
can be controlled by

C\epsilon(\kappa+1)\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

By recalling that

\mathcal{V}(\xi):=\left|w_{B}(\xi,0)\right|+\xi^{-1}\int_{1}^{\xi}|w_{F}(\eta,0)|d\eta+\epsilon\xi^{-3}|\psi(\xi,0)|+\epsilon^{2}\xi^{-1}\int_{1}^{\xi}\eta^{-3}|\psi(\eta,0)|d\eta,

\displaystyle\Psi(t_{*}):=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}|\partial_{t}\psi(1,t)|dt+\epsilon\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-4}|\psi(1,t)|dt

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t_{*}}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t_{*}-t)\right)^{-1}|\psi(1,t)|dt,

and classifying the terms on the right-hand side of (
6.22
), in view of
\kappa\leq\kappa_{0}\lesssim 1
, we obtain

\displaystyle v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\leq

\displaystyle\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|+C\epsilon\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))+C\epsilon\Psi(t_{*})

(6.26)

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

From (
6.21
) and in the same manner, there is also

\displaystyle\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(1,t_{*}\right)-\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0\right)\right|

(6.27)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))+C\epsilon\Psi(t_{*})+C\epsilon\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta\right)^{-1}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

Multiplying (
6.26
) by
\xi_{0}(t_{*})
yields

\displaystyle\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left(v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)-\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|\right)

(6.28)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left(\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))+\Psi(t_{*})\right)

\displaystyle+\epsilon\xi_{0}(t_{*})\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta\right)^{-1}\left(v_{B}(\eta,0)-\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\eta,0)\right|\right)d\eta

\displaystyle+\epsilon\xi_{0}(t_{*})\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta\right)^{-1}\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\eta,0)\right|d\eta.

By recalling that (
3.10
)(
3.11
), we obtain

\log\xi_{0}(t_{*})\leq\log\xi_{0}(T)\leq\log(1+\overline{c}T)\leq\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon},

(6.29)

and thus

\displaystyle\epsilon\xi_{0}(t_{*})\int_{1}^{\xi_{0}(t_{*})}\left(2\mu+\xi_{0}(t_{*})-\eta\right)^{-1}\eta^{-1}d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon\xi_{0}(t_{*})}{\xi_{0}(t_{*})+2\mu}\left(\log\xi_{0}(t_{*})+\log\frac{2\mu-1+\xi_{0}(t_{*})}{2\mu}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\kappa.

Therefore, taking maximum in (
6.28
) over
t_{*}\in[0,T]
yields

\displaystyle\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left(v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)-\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))+\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\Psi(t_{*})+\kappa\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|\right\}

\displaystyle+\kappa\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left(v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)-\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|\right)\right\}.

We remark here that since
\xi_{0}
is a bijection from
[0,T]
to
[1,\xi_{0}(T)]
it holds

\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))=\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi(T)]}\eta\mathcal{V}(\eta),

\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|\right\}=\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi(T)]}\left\{\eta\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\eta,0)\right|\right\}.

By choosing
\kappa_{0}
small, we arrive at

\displaystyle\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left(v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)-\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|\right)\right\}

(6.30)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))+\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\Psi(t_{*})+\kappa\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|\right\},

and it follows

\displaystyle\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)

(6.31)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))+\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\Psi(t_{*})+\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|\right\}.

Furthermore, multiplying (
6.27
) by
\xi_{0}(t_{*})
, taking maximum over
t_{*}\in[0,T]
and applying the bound (
6.31
) to the
v_{B}
term gives

\displaystyle\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left(\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(1,t_{*}\right)-\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0\right)\right|\right)\right\}

(6.32)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}))+\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\Psi(t_{*})+\kappa\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t_{*})\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(t_{*}),0)\right|\right\},

which proves Proposition
6.16
.

6.5
The decay estimate of
\psi(1,t)
.

Recall from (
5.4
)(
5.5
) that

W(s)=\left[aw_{B}|_{x=0},aw_{B}|_{x=0}\right]^{T}(s)
and that
a:=\left.\left(\frac{\tilde{f}^{\prime}(R)}{c}-\frac{\rho\partial_{t}u}{c}\right)\right|_{x=0}
with
\left|a(t)-\frac{\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)}{c_{0}}\right|\lesssim\epsilon
. We write

\displaystyle aw_{B}|_{x=0}(s)=

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)}{c_{0}}w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)+\frac{\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)}{c_{0}}\left(w_{B}(1,s)-w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right)+\left(a(s)-\frac{\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)}{c_{0}}\right)w_{B}(1,s).

The last two terms on the right-hand side are regarded as perturbation, and from (
6.31
)(
6.32
) they satisfy

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(\Lambda_{i}(t-s)\right)\left(w_{B}(1,s)-w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right)ds\right|

(6.33)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}(t-s)\right)\xi_{0}(s)^{-1}ds\right)\left(\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\Psi(s)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\Psi(s)\right),

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(\Lambda_{i}(t-s)\right)\left(a(s)-\frac{\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)}{c_{0}}\right)w_{B}(1,s)ds\right|

(6.34)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\left(\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}(t-s)\right)\xi_{0}(s)^{-1}ds\right)\left(\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\Psi(s)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon^{2}\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\epsilon^{2}\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\Psi(s)\right),

where
i=1,2
and we used (
3.10
) and
\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}<0
to deduce the inequality

\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(\Lambda_{i}(t-s)\right)\xi_{0}(s)^{-1}ds\leq\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}(t-s)\right)\left(1+\underline{c}s\right)^{-1}ds\lesssim\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}.

Using the above inequality again, we control the second term on the right-hand side of (
5.6
) by

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left[\begin{matrix}\Lambda_{1}(t-s)&\\
&\Lambda_{2}(t-s)\end{matrix}\right]\left(\Delta Y\right)(s)ds\right|

(6.35)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\left(\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left[\max_{i=1,2}\left\{\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}\right\}(t-s)\right]\left(1+\underline{c}s\right)^{-1}(s)ds\right)\left(\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)\left(|\partial_{t}\psi(1,s)|+|\psi(1,s)|\right)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)\left(|\partial_{t}\psi(1,s)|+|\psi(1,s)|\right)\right)

To eliminate the
\Psi(s)
terms in (
6.33
)(
6.34
) by
\psi(1,t)
and
\partial_{t}\psi(1,t)
, we recall the notation (
1.19
) and compute

\displaystyle\xi_{0}(t)\Psi(t)

(6.36)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\xi_{0}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}|\mathcal{R}(s)|ds

\displaystyle+\xi_{0}(t)\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}(1+\underline{c}s)^{-1}ds\right)\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|\partial_{t}\psi(1,s)-\mathcal{R}(s)|

\displaystyle+\epsilon\xi_{0}(t)\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-4}(1+\underline{c}s)^{-1}ds\right)\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|\psi(1,s)|

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\xi_{0}(t)\left(\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}(1+\underline{c}s)^{-1}ds\right)\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|\psi(1,s)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi_{0}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}|\mathcal{R}(s)|ds+\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|\partial_{t}\psi(1,s)-\mathcal{R}(s)|

\displaystyle+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|\psi(1,s)|+\epsilon\kappa\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|\psi(1,s)|.

Now applying (
6.33
-
6.36
) to (
5.6
) yields

\displaystyle\left|Y(t)-Y_{0}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left\{\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\epsilon\xi_{0}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}|\mathcal{R}(s)|ds+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|\partial_{t}\psi(s)-\mathcal{R}(s)|\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\epsilon^{2}\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|\psi(s)|\right\},

where
Y_{0}(t)
denotes the principle part of
Y(t)
, namely

Y_{0}(t):=\exp\left[\begin{matrix}\Lambda_{1}t&\\
&\Lambda_{2}t\end{matrix}\right]Y(0)-\frac{\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)}{c_{0}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left[\begin{matrix}\Lambda_{1}(t-s)&\\
&\Lambda_{2}(t-s)\end{matrix}\right]\left[\begin{matrix}w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\\
w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\end{matrix}\right]ds.

This automatically yields

\mathcal{R}(t)=\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}&-\frac{\Lambda_{2}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}\end{matrix}\right]Y_{0}(t),\quad\partial_{t}\psi(t)-\mathcal{R}(t)=\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}&-\frac{\Lambda_{2}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}\end{matrix}\right](Y(t)-Y_{0}(t)).

Then the above inequality can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\left|Y(t)-Y_{0}(t)\right|

(6.37)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left\{\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\epsilon\xi_{0}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}|Y_{0}(s)|ds+\epsilon^{2}\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|Y_{0}(s)|\right.

\displaystyle\left.+(\kappa+\epsilon^{2})\max_{s\in[0,t]}(1+\underline{c}s)|Y(s)-Y_{0}(s)|\right\}.

Multiplying (
6.37
) by
1+\underline{c}t
and taking maximum over
[0,T]
yields

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[0,T]}(1+\underline{c}t)|Y(t)-Y_{0}(t)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon^{2}\max_{s\in[0,T]}(1+\underline{c}s)|Y_{0}(s)|

\displaystyle+\epsilon\max_{t\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}|Y_{0}(s)|ds\right\}

\displaystyle+(\kappa+\epsilon^{2})\max_{t\in[0,T]}(1+\underline{c}t)|Y(t)-Y_{0}(t)|.

For small enough
\kappa_{0}
and
\epsilon_{0}
, this shows

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[0,T]}(1+\underline{c}t)|Y(t)-Y_{0}(t)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))

\displaystyle+\epsilon\max_{t\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}|Y_{0}(s)|ds\right\}+\epsilon^{2}\max_{s\in[0,T]}(1+\underline{c}s)|Y_{0}(s)|.

For the last two terms, we have

\displaystyle|Y_{0}(t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\left(\max_{i=1,2}\left\{\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}\right\}t\right)|Y(0)|

(6.38)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(\max_{i=1,2}\left\{\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}\right\}(t-s)\right)\xi_{0}(s)^{-1}ds\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\left(\max_{i=1,2}\left\{\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}\right\}t\right)|Y(0)|+\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\},

and thus

\displaystyle\epsilon\max_{t\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}|Y_{0}(s)|ds\right\}+\epsilon^{2}\max_{s\in[0,T]}(1+\underline{c}s)|Y_{0}(s)|

(6.39)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon|Y(0)|+\epsilon^{2}\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}

\displaystyle+\epsilon\max_{t\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}\left(1+\underline{c}s\right)^{-1}ds\right\}\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon|Y(0)|+(\kappa+\epsilon^{2})\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}.

Here we used that

\displaystyle\xi_{0}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}(t-s)\right)^{-1}\left(1+\underline{c}s\right)^{-1}ds=\xi_{0}(t)\frac{2}{3\underline{c}+\underline{c}^{2}t}\left(\log(1+\underline{c}t)+\log\left(1+\frac{\underline{c}}{2}t\right)\right)\lesssim\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}.

Therefore, by noting that
|w_{B}(\eta)|\leq\mathcal{V}(\eta)
,

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[0,T]}(1+\underline{c}t)|Y(t)-Y_{0}(t)|

(6.40)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\kappa+\epsilon^{2})\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon|Y(0)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon|Y(0)|.

By substituting the last three terms on the right-hand side of (
6.37
) by the bounds in (
6.39
)(
6.40
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left|Y(t)-Y_{0}(t)\right|

(6.41)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left\{\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\},

which proves Proposition
6.17
.

7
Close the bootstrap

With the help of the bounds obtained in the previous sections, we shall complete the bootstrap argument in this section.

7.1
Proof of almost global existence

To prove the bound (
3.12
), recall the energy identity (
2.34
), and we obtain by dropping the iteration subscripts that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left[\int_{0}^{\infty}((\partial_{t}^{j}u)^{2}+c^{2}(\partial_{t}^{j}q)^{2})dx+(c^{2}r^{2})|_{x=0}f^{\prime}(R)^{-1}|\partial_{t}^{j}f(R)|^{2}\right]=\sum_{l=1}^{5}nl_{l}^{j}(t),

(7.1)

where we denote

nl_{1}^{j}(t):=\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}c^{2}(\partial_{t}^{j}q)^{2}-r^{2}\partial_{x}c^{2}\partial_{t}^{j}u\partial_{t}^{j}q\right)dx,

nl_{2}^{j}(t):=\int_{0}^{\infty}[\partial_{t}^{j},c^{2}r^{2}\partial_{x}]q\partial_{t}^{j}udx,\quad nl_{3}^{j}(t):=\int_{0}^{\infty}c^{2}[\partial_{t}^{j},\partial_{x}r^{2}]u_{k}\partial_{t}^{j}qdx,

nl_{4}^{j}(t):=\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\left[(c^{2}r^{2})|_{x=0}f^{\prime}(R)^{-1}\right]|\partial_{t}^{j}f(R)|^{2},

nl_{5}^{j}(t):=-(c^{2}r^{2})|_{x=0}[\partial_{t}^{j},f^{\prime}(R)^{-1}]\partial_{t}f(R)\partial_{t}^{j}f(R).

From (
4.8
-
4.12
) and (
3.7
-
3.9
), it follows

\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left(|\partial_{t}^{j}u|^{2}+|\partial_{t}^{j}q|^{2}\right)\simeq\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left(|\partial_{t}^{j+1}\varphi|^{2}+|cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi|^{2}\right),

(7.2)

and thus

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left\{\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}\xi^{-1}\left((\partial_{t}^{1+j}\varphi)^{2}+(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi)^{2}\right)dxdt+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}\xi^{-3}\left(\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi\right)^{2}dxdt\right\}

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}\xi^{-1}\left((\partial_{t}^{j}u)^{2}+(\partial_{t}^{j}q)^{2}\right)dxdt,

with which we further deduce from (
4.1
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}\xi^{-1}\left((\partial_{t}^{j}u)^{2}+(\partial_{t}^{j}q)^{2}\right)dxdt\lesssim\max\left\{\log_{2}T,1\right\}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt

(7.3)

Using the above inequality with (
3.7
)(
3.11
), we estimate

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}|nl_{1}^{j}(t)|dt\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\xi\partial_{t}c^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\xi r^{2}\partial_{x}c^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}\xi^{-1}\left((\partial_{t}^{j}u)^{2}+(\partial_{t}^{j}q)^{2}\right)dxdt

(7.4)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\left(\max\left\{\log_{2}T,1\right\}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\kappa\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt.

Next, we compute

[\partial_{t},r^{2}c^{2}\partial_{x}]q=\partial_{t}(r^{2}c^{2})\partial_{x}q=\frac{\partial_{t}(c^{2}r^{2})}{c^{2}r^{2}}\partial_{t}u,

\displaystyle[\partial_{t}^{2},r^{2}c^{2}\partial_{x}]q=[\partial_{t}^{2},r^{2}c^{2}]\partial_{x}q=[\partial_{t}^{2},r^{2}c^{2}](r^{-2}c^{-2}\partial_{t}u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\partial_{t}(r^{2}c^{2})}{r^{2}c^{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}u+\frac{\partial_{t}^{2}(r^{2}c^{2})}{r^{2}c^{2}}\partial_{t}u+2\partial_{t}(r^{2}c^{2})\partial_{t}(r^{-2}c^{-2})\partial_{t}u,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{t}^{2}(r^{2}c^{2})}{r^{2}c^{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{t}^{2}c^{2}}{c^{2}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}r^{2}\partial_{t}c^{2}}{r^{2}c^{2}}+\frac{\partial_{t}^{2}r^{2}}{r^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\gamma+1)\rho\partial_{t}^{2}q+(\gamma+1)(\gamma+2)\rho^{2}(\partial_{t}q)^{2}+2\frac{\partial_{t}u}{r}+2\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}+8\frac{u\partial_{t}c}{rc}.

Hence by (
3.6
)(
3.7
),

\displaystyle\left|[\partial_{t}^{2},r^{2}c^{2}\partial_{x}]q\right|\lesssim\epsilon\xi^{-1}\left(|\partial_{t}^{2}u|+|\partial_{t}^{2}q|+|\partial_{t}u|\right).

From (
7.3
)(
3.11
), it follows

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}|nl_{2}^{j}(t)|dt\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\sum_{i=0}^{j}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}\xi^{-1}\left((\partial_{t}^{j}u)^{2}+(\partial_{t}^{j}q)^{2}\right)dxdt

(7.5)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\kappa\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt.

Similarly, to control
nl_{3}^{j}
, we compute

[\partial_{t},\partial_{x}r^{2}]u=\partial_{x}[\partial_{t},r^{2}]u=\partial_{x}(2ru^{2})=4\frac{u}{r}\partial_{t}q-6\frac{u^{2}}{\rho r^{2}},

\displaystyle[\partial_{t}^{2},\partial_{x}r^{2}]u=\partial_{x}[\partial_{t}^{2},r^{2}]u=\partial_{x}[2u^{3}+4ru\partial_{t}u]=\partial_{x}\left[4\frac{u}{r}\partial_{t}(r^{2}u)-6u^{3}\right],

\displaystyle\partial_{x}\left[\frac{u}{r}\partial_{t}(r^{2}u)\right]=

\displaystyle\frac{u}{r}\partial_{t}^{2}q+4\partial_{x}\left(\frac{u}{r}\right)\partial_{t}(r^{2}u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{u}{r}\partial_{t}^{2}q+r^{-1}\left(\partial_{t}q-3\rho^{-1}r^{-1}u\right)\left(\partial_{t}u+2r^{-1}u^{2}\right),

\partial_{x}u^{3}=3\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\left(\partial_{t}q-2\rho^{-1}r^{-1}u\right).

By (
7.3
)(
3.11
), we then obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}|nl_{3}^{j}(t)|dt\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\sum_{i=0}^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}\xi^{-1}\left((\partial_{t}^{j}u)^{2}+(\partial_{t}^{j}q)^{2}\right)dxdt

(7.6)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\kappa\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt.

Recall that
f(R)=q|_{x=0}
and
\rho^{\gamma-1}-1=-\left(\frac{Ca\gamma}{2(\gamma-1)}\right)^{-1}\left(\partial_{x}\varphi-\frac{1}{2}u^{2}\right)
, from which we write

q=\rho^{-1}-1=-\frac{\rho^{-1}-1}{\rho^{\gamma-1}-1}\left(\frac{Ca\gamma}{2(\gamma-1)}\right)^{-1}\left(\partial_{x}\varphi-\frac{1}{2}u^{2}\right).

In view of (
3.6
)(
3.7
), this shows

\displaystyle|f(R)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left.\left(\partial_{t}\varphi-\frac{1}{2}u^{2}\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|\leq\left|\partial_{t}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|+C\epsilon\left|\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\left.\partial_{t}\varphi\right|_{x=0}\right|+C\epsilon\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+C\epsilon|\varphi|_{x=0}|.

For derivatives of
f(R)
, we have

\partial_{t}q=-(\gamma-1)^{-1}\rho^{-\gamma}\partial_{t}\left(\rho^{\gamma-1}-1\right)=\left(\frac{Ca\gamma}{2}\right)^{-1}\rho^{-\gamma}\left(\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi-u\partial_{t}u\right),

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}f(R)|\lesssim

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi|_{x=0}|+C\epsilon\left|\partial_{t}u|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi|_{x=0}|+C\epsilon\left|\partial_{t}\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi|_{x=0}|+C\epsilon\left|\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|+C\epsilon\left|\left.\frac{\partial_{t}(\rho r^{2})}{\rho r^{2}}\right|_{x=0}\right|\cdot\left|\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi|_{x=0}|+C\epsilon\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\partial_{t}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+C\epsilon\left|\partial_{t}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+C\epsilon|\varphi_{x=0}|.

Similarly, by separating the top derivatives and using (
3.6
)(
3.7
), we continue and obtain

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}^{2}f(R)|=

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}^{2}q|_{x=0}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{Ca\gamma}{2}\right)^{-1}\left|\left.\partial_{t}\left(\rho^{-\gamma}\left(\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi-u\partial_{t}u\right)\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\left.\left(\partial_{t}^{3}\varphi-(\partial_{t}u)^{2}-u\partial_{t}^{2}u\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+\left|\left.\partial_{t}\rho^{-\gamma}\right|_{x=0}\right|\cdot\left|\left.\left(\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi-u\partial_{t}u\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\left.\left(\partial_{t}^{3}\varphi-(\partial_{t}u)^{2}-u\partial_{t}^{2}u\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle+\epsilon\left(\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}^{3}\varphi|_{x=0}|+\epsilon|\partial_{t}^{2}u|_{x=0}|+\epsilon\left(\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|\right),

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}^{2}u|_{x=0}|=

\displaystyle\left|\left.\partial_{t}^{2}\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi+2\partial_{t}(\rho r^{2})\partial_{x}\partial_{t}\varphi+\partial_{t}^{2}(\rho r^{2})\partial_{x}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+C\epsilon\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\left.\left(\rho^{-1}\partial_{t}^{2}\rho+4\frac{u}{\rho r}\partial_{t}\rho+\frac{2u^{2}}{r^{2}}+\frac{2\partial_{t}u}{r}\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|\cdot\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle+\epsilon\left(\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+|\partial_{t}^{2}\rho|_{x=0}|+|\partial_{t}\rho|_{x=0}|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

\displaystyle+\epsilon\left(\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+\left|\left.\left(\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+|\partial_{t}^{2}q|_{x=0}|+|\partial_{t}q|_{x=0}|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|+\epsilon|\partial_{t}^{2}q|_{x=0}|.

Combining the above two inequalities yields

|\partial_{t}^{2}f(R)|\lesssim|\partial_{t}^{3}\varphi|_{x=0}|+\epsilon\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+\epsilon\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|+\epsilon^{2}|\partial_{t}^{2}f(R)|.

For small enough
\epsilon
, this gives

|\partial_{t}^{2}f(R)|\lesssim|\partial_{t}^{3}\varphi|_{x=0}|+\epsilon\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+\epsilon\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|,

and thus for
j=0,1,2

|\partial_{t}^{j}f(R)|\lesssim|\partial_{t}^{1+j}\varphi|_{x=0}|+\epsilon\sum_{i=0}^{j}\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+\epsilon\sum_{i=0}^{j}\left|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|

(7.7)

We return to the estimate of
nl_{4}^{j}
. Since
c^{2}=c_{0}^{2}(1+q)^{-\gamma-1}
and
q|_{x=0}=f(R)
, there exists a function
f_{1}
which is smooth near
R=1
such that
(cr^{2})|_{x=0}f^{\prime}(R)^{-1}=f_{1}(R)
, and thus

\left|\partial_{t}\left[(cr^{2})|_{x=0}f^{\prime}(R)^{-1}\right]\right|=\left|\partial_{t}f_{1}(R)\right|=\left|f_{1}^{\prime}(R)\right||u|_{x=0}|\lesssim\epsilon.

It then follows from (
7.7
)(
4.1
)(
7.2
)(
3.11
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}|nl_{4}^{j}(t)|dt\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\left(\max\left\{\log_{2}T,1\right\}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt\right)

(7.8)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\kappa\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt.

The
nl_{5}^{j}
terms can also be treated by direct computation.

\left|[\partial_{t},f^{\prime}(R)^{-1}]\partial_{t}f(R)\cdot\partial_{t}f(R)\right|=\left|\partial_{t}(f^{\prime}(R)^{-1})\right|\cdot|\partial_{t}f(R)|^{2}\lesssim\epsilon|\partial_{t}f(R)|^{2},

[\partial_{t}^{2},f^{\prime}(R)^{-1}]\partial_{t}f(R)\cdot\partial_{t}^{2}f(R)=2\partial_{t}(f^{\prime}(R)^{-1})|\partial_{t}^{2}f(R)|^{2}+\partial_{t}^{2}(f^{\prime}(R))^{-1}\partial_{t}f(R)\partial_{t}^{2}f(R),

By Leibniz rule,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}(f^{\prime}(R)^{-1})=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{f^{\prime}}\circ f^{-1}\circ f(R)\right)=\partial_{t}\left[\left(\frac{1}{f^{\prime}}\circ f^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\circ f(R)\cdot\partial_{t}f(R)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{f^{\prime}}\circ f^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\circ f(R)\cdot\partial_{t}^{2}f(R)+\left(\frac{1}{f^{\prime}}\circ f^{-1}\right)^{\prime\prime}\circ f(R)\cdot(\partial_{t}f(R))^{2}.

Since
|\partial_{t}R|=|u|_{x=0}|\lesssim\epsilon
, it follows

\left|[\partial_{t}^{2},f^{\prime}(R)^{-1}]\partial_{t}f(R)\cdot\partial_{t}^{2}f(R)\right|\lesssim\epsilon\left((\partial_{t}^{2}f(R))^{2}+(\partial_{t}f(R))^{2}\right),

and by (
7.7
)(
4.1
)(
3.11
),

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}|nl_{5}^{j}(t)|dt\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\left(\left|\partial_{t}^{1+j}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|+\epsilon\sum_{i=0}^{j}\left|\left.\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|+\epsilon\sum_{i=0}^{j}\left|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi|_{x=0}\right|\right)

(7.9)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\left(\max\left\{\log_{2}T,1\right\}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\kappa\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt.

Integrating (
7.1
) over
[0,T]
and using (
7.4
)(
7.5
)(
7.6
)(
7.8
)(
7.9
) yields

e^{j}(t)-e^{j}(0)\lesssim\kappa\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt,

and it follows by taking maximum

\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)\leq\sum_{j=0}^{2}e^{j}(0)+C\kappa\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)+C\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt.

Hence for small enough
\kappa_{0}
and
\kappa\leq\kappa_{0}
such that
C\kappa_{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we obtain

\sum_{j=0}^{2}\max_{0\leq t\leq T}e^{j}(t)\leq 2\sum_{j=0}^{2}e^{j}(0)+C\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt.

(7.10)

To prove (
3.12
), it remains to bound
\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt
. To this end, we call upon Proposition
6.17
and use the following lemma.

Lemma 7.22
.

Under the condition (
1.20
) and for
\xi\geq 1
, we have the bounds

|Y(0)|\lesssim\epsilon,\quad\int_{1}^{\infty}|w_{B}(\eta,0)|^{2}d\eta\lesssim\epsilon^{2},

\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\left\{\eta\left|w_{B}(\eta,0)\right|\right\}\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}},\quad\max_{\xi\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\int_{1}^{\xi}|w_{F}(\eta,0)|d\eta\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),

\xi^{-2}|\psi(\xi,0)|\lesssim\epsilon\xi^{-1},\quad\int_{1}^{\xi}\eta^{-3}|\psi(\eta,0)|d\eta\lesssim\epsilon.

In particular,

\epsilon\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\eta\mathcal{V}(\eta)\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon^{2}.

Proof.

Recall that
\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi=u
. By the bootstrap bound (
3.2
)(
3.8
) at
t=0
and a Hölder inequality, it holds

|\varphi(x,0)|\leq\int_{x}^{\infty}\left|(1+q_{in})\frac{u_{in}}{r_{in}^{2}}(y)\right|dy\lesssim\|u_{in}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\epsilon.

From (
1.17
), we also have

|\partial_{t}\varphi(x,0)|\lesssim\|u_{in}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|q_{in}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon.

From
\psi=r\varphi
,
\partial_{t}\psi=r\partial_{t}\varphi+u\varphi
, and
r_{in}|_{\xi=1}=R_{in}\simeq 1
, it follows that

|Y(0)|\lesssim|\partial_{t}\psi|_{\left\{\xi=1,t=0\right\}}|+|\psi|_{\left\{\xi=1,t=0\right\}}|\lesssim|\partial_{t}\varphi|_{\left\{x=0,t=0\right\}}|+|\varphi|_{\left\{x=0,t=0\right\}}|\lesssim\epsilon,

\xi^{-2}|\psi(\xi,0)|=\xi^{-2}r_{in}(x)|\varphi(x,0)|\lesssim\epsilon\xi^{-1},

\int_{1}^{\xi}\eta^{-3}|\psi(\eta,0)|d\eta\lesssim\epsilon\int_{1}^{\xi}\eta^{-2}d\eta\lesssim\epsilon.

Using (
3.2
)(
3.8
) again, we compute

\displaystyle\xi|w_{B}(\xi,0)|=

\displaystyle\xi\left|(\partial_{t}+cr^{2}\partial_{x})(r\varphi)(x,0)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\xi r_{in}(x)\left(|\partial_{t}\varphi(x,0)|+|cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi(x,0)|\right)+\xi\left|\left(u_{in}(x)+\frac{c(x,0)}{\rho_{in}(x)}\right)\varphi(x,0)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{2}(|q_{in}(x)|+|u_{in}(x)|)+\xi|\varphi(x,0)|.

Similarly,

\xi|w_{F}(\xi,0)|\lesssim\xi^{2}(|q_{in}(x)|+|u_{in}(x)|)+\xi|\varphi(x,0)|.

Then by condition (
1.20
),

\displaystyle\xi^{2}|u_{in}(x)|\leq\left(2\int_{0}^{\infty}\xi^{2}|u_{in}(\xi)|\left|\partial_{x}(\xi^{2}u_{in})\right|dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\|\tilde{L}_{0}^{1}u_{in}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\xi^{2}u_{in}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\xi^{2}|q_{in}(x)|\leq\left(2\int_{0}^{\infty}\xi^{2}|q_{in}(\xi)|\left|\partial_{x}(\xi^{2}q_{in})\right|dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\left(\|L_{0}^{1}q_{in}\|_{L^{2}}+\|q_{in}\|_{L^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\xi^{2}q_{in}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\xi|\varphi(x,0)|\leq\xi\int_{x}^{\infty}(1+q_{in})\frac{|u_{in}|}{r_{in}^{2}}(y)dy\lesssim\xi\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{x}^{\infty}(1+3y)^{-\frac{4}{3}}dy\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Hence we obtain
\xi|w_{B}(\xi,0)|\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}
. Next, for the bound on
\int_{1}^{\infty}|w_{B}(\eta,0)|^{2}d\eta
, we use a change of variable and Hardy inequality to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\infty}|w_{B}(\eta,0)|^{2}d\eta\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\infty}\left(|\eta q_{in}(x(\eta))|^{2}+|\eta u_{in}(x(\eta))|^{2}+|\varphi(x(\eta),0)|^{2}\right)d\eta

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(|q_{in}(x)|^{2}+|u_{in}(x)|^{2}+|\xi^{-1}\varphi(x,0)|^{2}\right)dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|u_{in}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|q_{in}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}.

It remains to control
\max\limits_{\xi\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\int_{1}^{\xi}|w_{F}(\eta,0)|d\eta
, for which we use

\displaystyle\max_{\xi\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\int_{1}^{\xi}|w_{F}(\eta,0)|d\eta\lesssim\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta w_{F}(\eta,0)|^{2}d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta^{2}q_{in}(x(\eta))|^{2}d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta^{2}u_{in}(x(\eta))|^{2}d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta\varphi(x(\eta),0)|^{2}d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

By a change of variable and Hölder,

\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta^{2}q_{in}(x(\eta))|^{2}d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=\|\xi q_{in}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|q_{in}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\xi^{2}q_{in}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}},

and similarly,

\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta^{2}u_{in}(x(\eta))|^{2}d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

For the last term, integration by parts gives

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta\varphi(x(\eta),0)|^{2}d\eta\lesssim

\displaystyle|\varphi(0,0)|^{2}+\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta\varphi(x(\eta),0)||\eta^{2}\partial_{\eta}\varphi(x(\eta),0)|d\eta

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\varphi(0,0)|^{2}+\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta\varphi(x(\eta),0)|^{2}d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta^{2}u_{in}(x(\eta),0)|^{2}d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

where we used that
\rho r^{2}\simeq\xi^{2}
by (
3.2
)(
3.8
). Hence by Cauchy-Schwartz

\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta\varphi(x(\eta),0)|^{2}d\eta\lesssim\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon\tilde{\epsilon}.

Collecting the above bounds yields

\max_{\xi\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\int_{1}^{\xi}|w_{F}(\eta,0)|d\eta\lesssim\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}|\eta w_{F}(\eta,0)|^{2}d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right).

∎

Now we combine Proposition
6.17
with Lemma
7.1
in view of
\epsilon\leq\epsilon_{0}
,
\tilde{\epsilon}\leq\tilde{\epsilon}_{0}
,
\kappa\leq\kappa_{0}
to deduce

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{T}|Y_{0}(t)|^{2}dt+C\left(\epsilon|Y(0)|+\kappa\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\left\{\eta\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\eta,0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\eta\mathcal{V}(\eta)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{T}|Y_{0}(t)|^{2}dt+C\left(\epsilon^{2}+\kappa\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{T}|Y_{0}(t)|^{2}dt+C\epsilon\left(\epsilon_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\kappa_{0}\tilde{\epsilon}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon_{0}\tilde{\epsilon}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}.

Meanwhile, by Young inequality, Lemma
6.21
and Lemma
7.22
again we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(\max_{i=1,2}\left\{\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}\right\}(t-s)\right)|w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)|ds\right\|_{L^{2}[0,T]}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}|w_{B}(\xi_{0}(t),0)|^{2}dt\lesssim\int_{0}^{\xi_{0}(T)}|w_{B}(\eta,0)|^{2}d\eta\lesssim\epsilon^{2},

and thus
\int_{0}^{T}|Y_{0}(t)|^{2}dt\lesssim\epsilon^{2}
, which further gives

C\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt\leq C\epsilon^{2}\left(\epsilon_{0}+\left(\epsilon_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\kappa_{0}\tilde{\epsilon}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon_{0}\tilde{\epsilon}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}\right).

Hence for sufficiently small
\epsilon_{0},\tilde{\epsilon}_{0},\kappa_{0}
such that
C\left(\epsilon_{0}+\left(\epsilon_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\kappa_{0}\tilde{\epsilon}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon_{0}\tilde{\epsilon}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}\right)\leq\tilde{B}
, we obtain

C\epsilon\int_{0}^{T}\varphi^{2}|_{x=0}dt\leq\tilde{B}\epsilon^{2},

which together with (
7.10
) yields (
3.12
). Therefore, the solution can be continued up to
[0,T_{\epsilon}]
with
1+\overline{c}T_{\epsilon}\geq\exp\left(\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\right)
for all
\kappa\leq\kappa_{0}
. In particular, choosing
\kappa=\kappa_{0}
gives
T\geq T_{0}
.

7.2
Estimate of bubble radius

Now assume that
1+\overline{c}T=\exp\left(\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\right)
for
\kappa\leq\kappa_{0}
and
t\in[0,T]
.
To retrieve an estimate of
R(t)
, recall the relation (
1.17
). For simplicity, denote

g(q):=-\frac{Ca\gamma}{2(\gamma-1)}\left((1+q)^{1-\gamma}-1\right)

(7.11)

so that

g(q)=\partial_{t}\varphi-\frac{1}{2}u^{2}=r^{-1}\partial_{t}\psi-\frac{u}{r^{2}}\psi-\frac{1}{2}u^{2}.

From the boundary condition (
1.12
), it follows that

F(R)=\partial_{t}\psi|_{x=0}-\left.\left(\frac{u}{r}\psi+\frac{1}{2}ru^{2}\right)\right|_{x=0},

(7.12)

where

F(R)=R\cdot g\circ f(R).

(7.13)

Write

\partial_{t}\psi|_{x=0}=\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}\left.\left(\partial_{t}\psi-\Lambda_{2}\psi\right)\right|_{x=0}-\frac{\Lambda_{2}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}\left.\left(\partial_{t}\psi-\Lambda_{1}\psi\right)\right|_{x=0},

and from (
6.41
) we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{t}\psi|_{x=0}-\mathcal{R}(t)\right|

(7.14)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left\{\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\kappa\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),

where we used Lemma
7.22
and recall that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}(t):=

\displaystyle\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}e^{\Lambda_{1}t}\left.\left(\partial_{t}\psi-\Lambda_{2}\psi\right)\right|_{\left\{x=0,t=0\right\}}-\frac{\Lambda_{2}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}e^{\Lambda_{2}t}\left.\left(\partial_{t}\psi-\Lambda_{1}\psi\right)\right|_{\left\{x=0,t=0\right\}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)}{c_{0}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}e^{\Lambda_{1}(t-s)}-\frac{\Lambda_{2}}{\Lambda_{1}-\Lambda_{2}}e^{\Lambda_{2}(t-s)}\right)w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)ds.

To control the nonlinearity
\left.\left(\frac{u}{r}\psi+\frac{1}{2}ru^{2}\right)\right|_{x=0}
, we use

u=\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi=\rho r\partial_{x}\psi-r^{-2}\psi=\frac{\rho}{cr}\left(w_{B}-\partial_{t}\psi-\frac{c}{\rho r}\psi\right)

to obtain

\frac{u}{r}\psi+\frac{1}{2}ru^{2}=\frac{\rho^{2}}{2c^{2}r}\left(w_{B}-\partial_{t}\psi-\frac{c}{\rho r}\psi\right)\left(w_{B}-\partial_{t}\psi+\frac{c}{\rho r}\psi\right).

In view of (
3.2
)(
3.10
)(
6.38
)(
6.41
) and Lemma
7.22
, it holds

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{t}\psi|_{x=0}\right|+\left|\left.\left(\frac{c}{\rho r}\psi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|

(7.15)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|Y(t)-Y_{0}(t)|+|Y_{0}(t)|

\displaystyle\lesssim
