\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left\{\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}

\displaystyle+|Y_{0}(t)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\kappa\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right).

Meanwhile, by (
6.31
)(
6.32
)

\displaystyle|w_{B}(1,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\eta\mathcal{V}(\eta)+\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\Psi(t_{*})+\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\left\{\eta\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\eta,0)\right|\right\}\right).

From (
6.36
)(
6.39
)(
6.40
), we obtain

\displaystyle\epsilon\max_{t_{*}\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(t_{*})\Psi(t_{*})

(7.16)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon|Y(0)|+(\kappa+\epsilon^{2})\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+(\kappa+\epsilon^{2})\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s)).

In view of
\epsilon,\kappa\lesssim 1
, it follows

\displaystyle|w_{B}(1,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\eta\mathcal{V}(\eta)+\epsilon|Y(0)|+\max_{\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]}\left\{\eta\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\eta,0)\right|\right\}\right)

(7.17)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\right).

Combining (
7.15
)(
7.17
) yields

\left|\left.\left(\frac{u}{r}\psi+\frac{1}{2}ru^{2}\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon}{(1+\underline{c}t)^{2}}(\epsilon+\tilde{\epsilon}).

Therefore, (
7.12
)(
7.14
) give

\left|F(R)-\mathcal{R}(t)\right|\lesssim\frac{\kappa}{1+\underline{c}t}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\frac{\epsilon}{(1+\underline{c}t)^{2}}(\epsilon+\tilde{\epsilon}).

(7.18)

Now we set
T=t
so that
\kappa=\epsilon\log(1+\overline{c}t)
. Then (
7.18
) becomes

\left|F(R)-\mathcal{R}(t)\right|\lesssim\frac{\log(1+\overline{c}t)}{1+\underline{c}t}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\frac{\epsilon}{(1+\underline{c}t)^{2}}(\epsilon+\tilde{\epsilon}),

which is exactly the desired estimate of Theorem
1.3
.
If we assume additionally
w_{B}|_{t=0}=0
, then (
7.14
)(
7.15
)(
7.17
) can be improved to

\left|\partial_{t}\psi|_{x=0}-\mathcal{R}(t)\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{t}\psi|_{x=0}\right|+\left|\left.\left(\frac{c}{\rho r}\psi\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\right)+\exp\left(\max_{i=1,2}\left\{\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}\right\}t\right)\epsilon,

|w_{B}(1,t)|\lesssim\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\right).

Hence

\left|\left.\left(\frac{u}{r}\psi+\frac{1}{2}ru^{2}\right)\right|_{x=0}\right|\lesssim\left(\frac{1}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\exp\left(\max_{i=1,2}\left\{\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}\right\}t\right)\epsilon\right)^{2},

\left|F(R)-\mathcal{R}(t)\right|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\exp\left(2\max_{i=1,2}\left\{\text{Re}\Lambda_{i}\right\}t\right)\epsilon^{2}\lesssim\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}{1+\underline{c}t}\left(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\tilde{\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right).

7.3
Proof of Corollary
1.4

Now we assume additionally
u_{in}
,
q_{in}
are compactly supported in
[0,x_{b})
. From the relations
u=\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi
,
\psi=r\varphi
and (
1.17
), it follows
w_{B}(\cdot,0)=\left.\left(\partial_{t}+cr^{2}\xi^{-2}\partial_{\xi}\right)\psi\right|_{t=0}
is compactly supported in
\xi\in[1,\xi_{b})
with
\xi_{b}=(1+x_{b})^{\frac{1}{3}}
. Suppose
(x,t)\in D_{b}
with
D_{b}
given by (
1.21
). Define
T
such that
T=t+\frac{\xi-1}{\underline{c}}
if
t\geq\frac{1}{\underline{c}}
and
T=\frac{\xi}{\underline{c}}
if
t<\frac{1}{\underline{c}}
so that
T\leq T_{0}
and
(\xi,t)
lies in the backward acoustic cone determined by the point
(1,T)
on the time axis. Let
\kappa\leq\kappa_{0}
be such that
1+\overline{c}T=\exp\left(\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\right)
, so we always have
\kappa\geq\epsilon
.
Note that
\xi_{0}
is a bijection from
[0,T]
to
[1,\xi_{0}(T)]
. Hence taking (
7.16
) back to (
6.30
) yields that for
\eta\in[1,\xi_{0}(T)]

\displaystyle v_{B}(\eta,0)-\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\eta,0\right)\right|

(7.19)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta^{-1}\left\{\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}.

For any
t^{\prime}\leq T
with
\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\geq\xi_{b}
, using (
7.19
) in (
6.18
) gives that

\displaystyle v_{F}(1,t^{\prime};T)-\left|\frac{w_{F}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(1,t^{\prime})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\left|\frac{w_{F}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(1,t^{\prime})\right|+\epsilon^{2}\left|\psi(1,t^{\prime})\right|

\displaystyle+\epsilon\left(\max_{\eta\in[\xi_{0}(t^{\prime}),\xi_{0}(T)]}\eta v_{B}(\eta,0)\right)\int_{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}^{\xi_{0}(T)}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}\left(\eta-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\right)\right)^{-1}\eta^{-1}d\eta

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\left|\frac{w_{F}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(1,t^{\prime})\right|+\epsilon^{2}\left|\psi(1,t^{\prime})\right|+\epsilon\int_{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}^{\xi_{0}(T)}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}\left(\eta-\xi_{0}(t)\right)\right)^{-1}\eta^{-1}d\eta

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}.

We compute for
\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})>2\mu
to get

\displaystyle\int_{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}^{\xi_{0}(T)}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}\left(\eta-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\right)\right)^{-1}\eta^{-1}d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu}{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})-2\mu}\int_{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}^{\xi_{0}(T)}\left(\frac{1}{\eta-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})+2\mu}-\frac{1}{\eta}\right)d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu}{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})-2\mu}\log\frac{\left(\xi_{0}(T)-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})+2\mu\right)\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}{2\mu\xi_{0}(T)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu}{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})-2\mu}\log\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu\xi_{0}(T)}\left(\xi_{0}(T)-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\right)\left(\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})-2\mu\right)\right).

If
\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\in(2\mu,2\mu+1]
,

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu}{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})-2\mu}\log\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu\xi_{0}(T)}\left(\xi_{0}(T)-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\right)\left(\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})-2\mu\right)\right)\leq\frac{\xi_{0}(T)-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}{\xi_{0}(T)}\leq 1\lesssim\frac{1}{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}.

If
\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})>2\mu+1
, then

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu}{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})-2\mu}\log\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu\xi_{0}(T)}\left(\xi_{0}(T)-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\right)\left(\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})-2\mu\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu}{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})-2\mu}\log\left(1+\frac{(\xi_{0}(T)-2\mu)^{2}}{8\mu\xi_{0}(T)}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\frac{1}{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}.

For
\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\leq 2\mu
, the estimate is much simpler:

\int_{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}^{\xi_{0}(T)}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}\left(\eta-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\right)\right)^{-1}\eta^{-1}d\eta\leq 2\mu\int_{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}^{\xi_{0}(T)}\eta^{-2}d\eta\leq 2\mu\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})^{-1}.

Hence in each case we have

\int_{\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})}^{\xi_{0}(T)}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\mu}\left(\eta-\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})\right)\right)^{-1}\eta^{-1}d\eta\lesssim\frac{\kappa}{\epsilon}\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})^{-1},

and it follows

\displaystyle v_{F}(1,t^{\prime};T)-\left|\frac{w_{F}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(1,t^{\prime})\right|

(7.20)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon\left|\frac{w_{F}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(1,t^{\prime})\right|+\epsilon^{2}|\psi(1,t^{\prime})|

\displaystyle+\kappa\xi_{0}(t^{\prime})^{-1}\left\{\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}.

Since
w_{F}=-w_{B}+2\partial_{t}\psi
, it holds
|w_{F}(1,t^{\prime})|\leq|w_{B}(1,t^{\prime})|+2|\partial_{t}\psi(1,t^{\prime})|
, and thus

\left|\frac{w_{F}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(1,t^{\prime})\right|\lesssim v_{B}(1,t^{\prime})+|Y(t^{\prime})|=v_{B}(\xi_{0}(t^{\prime}),0)+|Y(t^{\prime})|.

(7.21)

Denote
\Lambda:=\max\left\{\text{Re}\Lambda_{1},\text{Re}\Lambda_{2}\right\}
. Since
w_{B}
is compactly supported in
[1,\xi_{b})
, we have
w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)=0
for
s\geq T_{b}
. It then follows from (
6.41
) that

\displaystyle|Y(t^{\prime})|\leq

\displaystyle e^{\Lambda t^{\prime}}\left(|Y(0)|+\frac{|\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)|}{c_{0}}\int_{0}^{T_{b}}e^{-\Lambda s}|w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)|ds\right)

(7.22)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{1+\underline{c}t^{\prime}}\left\{\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}.

Since
(x,t)\in D_{b}
,
1+\underline{c}t\geq\xi+\xi_{b}-1\geq\xi
, and thus there exists a unique
T_{\xi}(t)\in[t-\frac{\xi-1}{\underline{c}},t]
such that
X_{F}(1,T_{\xi}(t);t-T_{\xi}(t))=(\xi,t)
by continuity, cf. (figure
7
).
Since
u=\rho r^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi
and
\varphi=r^{-1}\psi
, it holds
u=\rho r\partial_{x}\psi-r^{-2}\psi
. In view of
r\simeq\xi
and
\underline{c}\leq c\leq\overline{c}
, it follows

\displaystyle|u(x,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}\left(|w_{B}(\xi,t)|+|w_{F}(\xi,t)|\right)+\xi^{-2}|\psi(\xi,t)|

(7.23)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}\left(v_{B}(\xi,t)+v_{F}(\xi,t;T)\right)+\xi^{-2}|\psi(\xi,t)|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}\left(v_{B}(X_{B}(\xi,t;-t))+v_{F}(1,T_{\xi}(t);T)\right)+\xi^{-2}|\psi(\xi,t)|.

To control
|\psi(\xi,t)|
, we recall from Subsection
6.1
that
X_{F}(1,t;\sigma_{1}(\eta,t))=X_{B}(\eta,0;t+\sigma_{1}(\eta,t))
and use Lemma
6.18
to get

\displaystyle|\psi(\xi,t)|=

\displaystyle|\psi(X_{F}(1,T_{\xi}(t);t-T_{\xi}(t))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\psi(1,T_{\xi}(t))|+\int_{0}^{t-T_{\xi}(t)}|w_{B}\left(X_{F}(1,T_{\xi}(t);\tau)\right)|d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|Y(T_{\xi}(t))|+\int_{0}^{t-T_{\xi}(t)}v_{B}\left(X_{F}(1,T_{\xi}(t);\tau)\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|Y(T_{\xi}(t))|+\int_{\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))}^{X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;-t)}v_{B}(\eta,0)d\eta.

Since
T_{\xi}(t)\geq t-\frac{\xi-1}{\underline{c}}
and
(x,t)\in D_{b}
, it holds

\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))\geq 1+\underline{c}T_{\xi}(t)\geq\underline{c}t+2-\xi\geq\xi_{b}.

Hence it follows from (
7.19
) and
w_{B}(\eta,0)=0
,
\eta\geq\xi_{b}
that

\displaystyle|\psi(\xi,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle|Y(T_{\xi}(t))|+\int_{\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))}^{X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;-t)}\eta^{-1}d\eta

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}.

We claim that for all
s\in[0,T_{\xi}(t)]
,

X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t-s)-X_{B}^{(\xi)}(1,T_{\xi}(t);-s)\leq e^{C\kappa}\left(X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t)-1\right).

Figure 7:
Position of the point
X_{B}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t)

In particular, taking
s=T_{\xi}(t)
yields

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;-t)-\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))\leq e^{C\kappa}\left(X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t)-1\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{C\kappa}\left(\xi-1+\overline{c}(t-T_{\xi}(t)\right)\leq e^{C\kappa}\frac{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}{\underline{c}}(\xi-1).

Note that
X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t-\tau)=X_{B}^{(\xi)}\left(X_{B}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t);-\tau\right)
. Using (
3.6
)(
3.7
) and a mean value formula, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t-\tau)-X_{B}^{(\xi)}(1,T_{\xi}(t);-\tau)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon X_{B}^{(\xi)}(1,T_{\xi}(t);-\tau)^{-1}\left(X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t-\tau)-X_{B}^{(\xi)}(1,T_{\xi}(t);-\tau)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon(1+\underline{c}\tau)^{-1}\left(X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t-\tau)-X_{B}^{(\xi)}(1,T_{\xi}(t);-\tau)\right).

Gronwall’s inequality then yields

\displaystyle X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t-s)-X_{B}^{(\xi)}(1,T_{\xi}(t);-s)\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left\{\int_{0}^{s}C\epsilon(1+\underline{c}\tau)^{-1}d\tau\right\}\left(X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t)-1\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{C\kappa}\left(X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;T_{\xi}(t)-t)-1\right),

which proves the claim. Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))}^{X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;-t)}\eta^{-1}d\eta\leq\log\left(1+\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))^{-1}e^{C\kappa}\frac{\overline{c}+\underline{c}}{\underline{c}}(\xi-1)\right)\lesssim\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))^{-1}(\xi-1),

and

\displaystyle|\psi(\xi,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle|Y(T_{\xi}(t))|+\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))^{-1}(\xi-1)

(7.24)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}.

Now we take
\eta=X_{B}(\xi,t;-t)
in (
7.19
),
t^{\prime}=T_{\xi}(t)
in (
7.20
), and collect the resulted bounds as well as (
7.24
). It then follows from (
7.23
) that

\displaystyle|u(x,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}\left|\frac{w_{F}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(1,T_{\xi}(t))\right|+\xi^{-2}|Y(T_{\xi}(t))|+\epsilon^{2}\xi^{-1}|\psi(1,T_{\xi}(t))|

\displaystyle+\left(\xi^{-1}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;-t)^{-1}+\xi^{-2}(\xi-1)\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))^{-1}+\kappa\xi^{-1}\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}.

Next, we proceed with the help of (
7.21
) and (
7.19
):

\displaystyle|u(x,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}|Y(T_{\xi}(t)|+\left(\xi^{-1}X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;-t)^{-1}+\xi^{-2}(\xi-1)\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))^{-1}+\xi^{-1}\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))^{-1}\right)

(7.25)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}|Y(T_{\xi}(t)|+\xi^{-1}\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))^{-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\kappa\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\right\},

where we used that
\xi^{-2}(\xi-1)\leq\xi^{-1}
and
\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))\leq X_{B}^{(\xi)}(\xi,t;-t)
.
Since
u_{in}
,
q_{in}
are compactly supported in
[1,x_{b})
, it holds
\tilde{\epsilon}\lesssim\xi_{b}^{2}\epsilon
. From Lemma
7.22
it follows

\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\lesssim\kappa\epsilon\xi_{b}+\epsilon^{2}\xi_{b}\lesssim\kappa\epsilon\xi_{b}.

In view of (
7.22
) and the facts that
T_{\xi}(t)\geq t-\frac{\xi-1}{\underline{c}}
,
\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t))\geq 1+\underline{c}T_{\xi}(t)\geq 2+\underline{c}t-\xi
, (
7.25
) gives

\displaystyle|u(x,t)|\lesssim\xi^{-1}e^{\Lambda(t-\frac{\xi-1}{\underline{c}})}\left(|Y(0)|+\frac{|\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)|}{c_{0}}\int_{0}^{T_{b}}e^{-\Lambda s}|w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)|ds\right)+\frac{\kappa\epsilon\xi_{b}}{\xi(2+\underline{c}t-\xi)}.

Hence for
t\geq\frac{1}{\underline{c}}
we obtain the desired bound of
u

\displaystyle|u(x,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}e^{\Lambda(t-\frac{\xi-1}{\underline{c}})}\left(|Y(0)|+\frac{|\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)|}{c_{0}}\int_{0}^{T_{b}}e^{-\Lambda s}|w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)|ds\right)

(7.26)

\displaystyle+\xi^{-1}(2+\underline{c}t-\xi)^{-1}\log\left(1+\overline{c}\left(t+\frac{\xi-1}{\underline{c}}\right)\right)\epsilon^{2}\xi_{b}.

If assume additionally
w_{B}|_{t=0}=0
, then

\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\xi_{0}(s)\mathcal{V}(\xi_{0}(s))+\epsilon\max_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{\xi_{0}(s)\left|\frac{w_{B}}{c^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\xi_{0}(s),0)\right|\right\}+\epsilon|Y(0)|\lesssim\epsilon^{2}\xi_{b},

and thus

\displaystyle|u(x,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}e^{\Lambda(t-\frac{\xi-1}{\underline{c}})}\left(|Y(0)|+\frac{|\tilde{f}^{\prime}(1)|}{c_{0}}\int_{0}^{T_{b}}e^{-\Lambda s}|w_{B}(\xi_{0}(s),0)|ds\right)

(7.27)

\displaystyle+\xi^{-1}(2+\underline{c}t-\xi)^{-1}\epsilon^{2}\xi_{b}.

In view of
\rho\simeq 1
,
|u|\lesssim\epsilon
, relation (
1.17
) and that
\partial_{t}\varphi=r^{-1}\partial_{t}\psi-\frac{u}{r^{2}}\psi
, we can bound
q
by

\displaystyle|q(x,t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}|\partial_{t}\psi(\xi,t)|+|u(x,t)|\left(|u(x,t)|+\xi^{-2}|\psi(\xi,t)|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}\left(|w_{B}(\xi,t)|+|w_{F}(\xi,t)|\right)+|u(x,t)|\left(|u(x,t)|+\xi^{-2}|\psi(\xi,t)|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\xi^{-1}\left(v_{B}(X_{B}(\xi,t;-t))+v_{F}(1,\xi_{0}(T_{\xi}(t);T)\right)+\xi^{-2}|\psi(\xi,t)|.

Hence
|q(x,t)|
can be controlled by the same bound as in (
7.26
) or (
7.27
).

Appendix A
Detailed calculation of identity (
4.3
)

In this appendix we write down the explicit calculation of (
4.3
), for which we consider the following model question.

Assume that
\varphi
satisfies for
x>0
,
t\in[0,T]
that

\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi-c^{2}\partial_{x}(r^{4}\partial_{x}\varphi)=F,

(A.1)

and the modified momentum density are defined as

P_{0}=A\partial_{t}\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi+\frac{1}{2}B\varphi\partial_{t}\varphi,

P_{1}=\frac{1}{2}A\left[\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\right)^{2}+\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2}B\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi-\frac{1}{4}cr^{2}\partial_{x}B\varphi^{2},

where
A
and
B
are undetermined weight functions.
We compute:

\displaystyle\partial_{x}\left(cr^{2}P_{1}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x}\left[Acr^{2}\left(\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\right)^{2}+\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{2}B\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{2}B\varphi\partial_{x}\left(c^{2}r^{4}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}cr^{2}\partial_{x}B\cdot\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi-\frac{1}{4}\partial_{x}\left(c^{2}r^{4}\partial_{x}B\right)\varphi^{2}-\frac{1}{2}cr^{2}\partial_{x}B\cdot\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x}\left(cr^{2}A\right)\left(\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\right)^{2}+\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{2}Acr^{2}\partial_{x}\left(\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\right)^{2}+\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}B\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{2}B\varphi\partial_{x}\left(c^{2}r^{4}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)-\frac{1}{4}\partial_{x}\left(c^{2}r^{4}\partial_{x}B\right)\varphi^{2},

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}P_{0}=

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\left(A\partial_{t}\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)-\frac{1}{2}(\partial_{t}B)\varphi\partial_{t}\varphi-\frac{1}{2}B\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{2}B\varphi\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\partial_{t}A)\partial_{t}\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi-A\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)-\frac{1}{2}B\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}B\varphi\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi-\frac{1}{2}(\partial_{t}B)\varphi\partial_{t}\varphi.

Note that by
A.1
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}cr^{2}\partial_{x}\left(\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\right)^{2}+\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}\right)-\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}\varphi-\partial_{t}\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)\right)+cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)-\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{t}(cr^{2})}{cr^{2}}\partial_{t}\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi+cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\left(\frac{c}{r^{2}}\right)r^{4}\partial_{x}\varphi-F\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-c\partial_{x}r^{2}\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}+r^{2}\partial_{x}c\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}-\frac{\partial_{t}(cr^{2})}{cr^{2}}\partial_{t}\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi-cr^{2}\partial_{x}\cdot F,

and

\displaystyle\partial_{x}\left(c^{2}r^{4}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)-\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi=2\left(r^{2}\partial_{x}c\right)cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi-F.

Hence

\displaystyle\partial_{x}\left(cr^{2}P_{1}\right)-\partial_{t}P_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\partial_{x}\left(cr^{2}A\right)}{2}-\frac{B}{2}\right)\left(\partial_{t}\varphi\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{\partial_{x}\left(cr^{2}A\right)}{2}-Ac\partial_{x}r^{2}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\partial_{x}\left(c^{2}r^{4}\partial_{x}B\right)\varphi^{2}+r^{2}\partial_{x}c\cdot A\left(cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi\right)^{2}-A\frac{\partial_{t}(cr^{2})}{cr^{2}}\partial_{t}\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi

\displaystyle+Br^{2}\partial_{x}c\cdot\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi-\left(Acr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi+\frac{1}{2}B\varphi\right)F-\partial_{t}A\cdot\partial_{t}\varphi\cdot cr^{2}\partial_{x}\varphi-\frac{\partial_{t}B}{2}\varphi\partial_{t}\varphi.

Therefore we obtain (
4.3
) by substituting
\varphi
by
\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi
,
F
by
[\partial_{t}^{j},c^{2}\partial_{x}r^{4}\partial_{x}]\varphi
,
A
by
M_{k}
and
B
by
N_{k}
in view of (
4.2
).
